TL;DR: Paper defines scientific-claim-level unlearning, releases SciUnlearn benchmark, and demonstrates existing unlearning approaches are insufficient for structured, interconnected, evolving scientific knowledge.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMs are trained on static scientific corpora while scientific knowledge evolves; outdated/retracted claims persist in models and can propagate misinformation. There is a need for methods to remove obsolete scientific claims from LMs (scientific claim unlearning).

Method: Designed the Scientific Claim Unlearning task and created SciUnlearn benchmark; evaluated existing unlearning techniques on claim-level forgetting and measured their effectiveness, finding they often fail to eliminate encoded claim knowledge beyond superficial suppression.

Result: Introduced the task of Scientific Claim Unlearning and built a benchmark (SciUnlearn). Empirical evaluation shows current unlearning methods fail to effectively remove claim-level structured knowledge, achieving only superficial suppression.

Conclusion: Specialized unlearning methods are required to remove structured scientific claims while retaining model utility; existing instance-level unlearning techniques do not suffice.

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are trained on static scientific corpora, whereas scientific knowledge continuously evolves through correction and revision. Scientific claims encoded within these models may later become retracted, disproven, or updated by subsequent research, creating the risk of disseminating outdated information in scientific workflows. This creates a need for LMs to forget obsolete scientific claims. Machine unlearning offers a promising solution by enabling knowledge removal while maintaining overall model utility. Existing studies primarily investigate instance-level forgetting; however, scientific claims introduce additional challenges because they are interconnected, and continually evolving. To address this gap, we introduce the task of Scientific Claim Unlearning and present a new benchmark, SciUnlearn. We show that current unlearning approaches are unable to effectively eliminate claim-level knowledge and often achieve only superficial suppression, highlighting the need for specialized methods designed for structured knowledge removal.

</details>


### [64] [TreeWY: Speculative Verification for Gated DeltaNet Hybrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20961)
*Sneha Murthy Ghantasala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用树状WY变换与伪值矩阵替代逐节点递归状态快照，解锁在投机解码下大幅节省内存并改善内存受限场景的吞吐与首字节延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代开放模型使用Gated DeltaNet(GDN)等混合层，保持小固定尺寸的递归状态以节省常规解码内存，但这在启发式/投机式解码（speculative decoding）场景要求对草稿位置回滚时，需要对每个位置做完整状态快照，导致内存不可控地增长，限制了草稿树的宽度与可行性。

Method: 基于gated delta规则推导一个树形WY变换，使得树上每个草稿节点的输出可通过单次三角求解得到；在提交（commit）时仅重建所接受分支的完整状态。实现上用伪值矩阵替代逐节点的完整状态快照，推导仅依赖于gated delta规则本身，与其他网络细节无关。

Result: 在Qwen3.5家族的35B和397B两种规模的服务基准上验证：在相同接受长度下显著降低了投机时的递归状态内存与KV缓存压力，释放出的HBM被用于提升吞吐并在内存受限时显著降低首字节时间（TTFT）；在非内存受限场景代价仅为数个百分点。更少的内存开销也允许更宽、更高接受率的草稿树成为可行，尽管目前宽树还未带来吞吐上的提升。

Conclusion: 论文提出用树结构的WY变换对“gated delta”规则进行变换，消除对每个草稿位置保存完整递归状态(snapshot)的需求，从而用一次三角求解计算每个草稿节点的输出，并只在提交时重建被接受的状态。这样每个节点只需存储小的伪值矩阵而非完整状态，显著减少了推理时的递归状态与KV缓存内存压力。

Abstract: Modern open models are hybrids: most layers are linear-attention (Gated DeltaNet, GDN) layers carrying a small fixed-size recurrent state instead of a growing key-value (KV) cache. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wide, high-acceptance tree becomes memory-infeasible. We remove the snapshots. Using a tree-structured WY transform of the gated delta rule, we compute every draft node's output with a single triangular solve and reconstruct only the one accepted state on commit, storing a small pseudo-value matrix instead of per-node states; the derivation depends only on the gated delta rule, not on any other architectural detail. In serving benchmarks on two scales of one hybrid model family (Qwen3.5 35B and 397B) this cuts speculative recurrent-state memory and KV-cache pressure at identical acceptance length, turning the freed HBM into higher throughput and much lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) wherever memory binds, and costing a few percent where it does not. For tree width the same memory buys affordability: a wider, higher-acceptance draft becomes possible, though not yet a throughput win.

</details>


### [65] [Generalizing Soft Tissue Deformation and Force Prediction Across Material Stiffness and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20967)
*Madina Kojanazarova,Sidaty El Hadramy,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在SOFA中对超弹性本构进行系统标定，并以此生成训练数据，作者训练了一个软度条件化的等变GNN，实现了对多刚度组织和未见几何的实时、准确形变与力预测；但力学预测依赖于标定质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的替代模型依赖于FEM生成的数据，但其可靠性受限于本构模型的标定；现有工作缺乏针对不同刚度水平的系统性模型选择指导，且难以在不同组织刚度与几何间泛化。作者希望通过系统标定与软度条件化的等变GNN弥补这些缺陷。

Method: 在SOFA框架中对多种刚度的重力加载硅胶梁进行全面的超弹性本构模型标定，利用标定后的FEM仿真生成训练数据；构建带柔软度条件的等变图神经网络（equivariant GNN），输入几何与柔软度条件后同时预测形变与力学响应，实现对多种组织类型及未见几何的泛化，并实现实时推理（≈0.01s）。

Result: 在硅胶梁数据上，模型在形变上达到亚毫米级平均误差，推理时间约0.010秒；展示了力预测性能与上游本构模型标定一致性直接相关，即标定越一致，力学预测质量越高。

Conclusion: 该工作把物理一致性的FEM标定与具有旋转/平移等变性的图神经网络结合起来，在实时软组织仿真领域取得了有竞争力的精度与速度，同时指出了上游标定对力学预测的关键影响。

Abstract: Accurate soft tissue simulation is essential for surgical training, pre-operative planning, and haptic feedback systems. While learning-based surrogate models trained on data using the finite element method (FEM) offer a promising path to real-time inference, their reliability depends on well-calibrated constitutive models. Existing approaches neither provide systematic guidance on model selection across stiffness levels, nor generalize across different tissue stiffnesses or geometries. We perform a comprehensive calibration of hyperelastic constitutive models in the SOFA Framework using gravity-loaded silicone beams with different stiffnesses. Using calibrated simulations as training data, we use a softness conditioned equivariant graph neural network, enabling deformation and force prediction across multiple tissue types and unseen geometries. Our model achieves sub-millimeter mean deformation accuracy at 0.010s inference time, while showing that force prediction quality is directly tied to upstream calibration consistency.

</details>


### [66] [Deep Learning Models Also Recall Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20970)
*Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者把范式从“事实回忆”推广为“特征回忆”，主张线性投影可被看作从权重中检索信息并由输入激活调制，提出辨识方法并指出可供后续实证验证的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大模型在权重中回忆事实的现象出发，作者认为这反映了一种更普遍的运算模式（不仅限于“事实”），希望为哲学和可解释性研究提供新概念并指引实证方向。

Method: 提出并形式化“特征回忆”（feature recall）的概念：把线性投影视为在权重中检索被存储的信息，并以输入激活作为缩放因子；对比传统的特征组合范式，扩展到不同架构，并讨论机制性识别的方法。

Result: 给出了概念定义与核心观察（线性投影可解读为检索并按激活缩放），展示该概念在多种架构中的适用性，提出可用于识别特征回忆的实验/机制性指标，并讨论与特征组合的区别及其哲学意义。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个简洁且具启发性的理论框架，将若干观测整合为可操作的概念；但要确立其普适性与可测性，需要补充更明确的数学定义、系统的实验证据（消除替代解释）、以及对非线性组件和训练动态的深入分析。

Abstract: Recent work in mechanistic interpretability has studied how large language models recall facts stored in their weights. This paper argues that factual recall points to something broader: a general kind of operation in deep learning models, which I call feature recall. The core observation is that a linear projection can be read as retrieving stored information scaled by input activations. I define feature recall, show it applies across architectures, and contrast it with the established paradigm of feature combination. I also consider how cases of feature recall might be mechanistically identified. The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [67] [Belief Without Behavior: Measuring the Translation of Theory of Mind into Coordinated Social Action in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20975)
*Tonglin Yan,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Rudrauf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSAIC 是一个检验“从心智推理到多模态社会行为”的基准。实验表明现有 VLM 不能把 ToM 推理转化为一致的多通道行为；显式 ToM-耦合结构（如 PCM-LLM）能成功完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于填补现有基准的空白：尽管有大量工作研究 ToM 推理或具身行为生成，但几乎没有基准把“从推理（心智模型）到同步多模态社会行为”的能力放在一起测评。MOSAIC 旨在衡量并推动此类整合能力的发展。

Method: 构建 MOSAIC 基准：控制的合作与竞争场景；多模态信号（语言陈述、空间轨迹、凝视、面部表情）；系统化改变 ToM-阶次约束；对 13 个模型（含 11 个 VLM）在每个模型上跑 200 次试验；信号层面做方向性和反应性分析；用 PCM-LLM 作为具有显式 ToM 模块的参考架构。

Result: MOSAIC 提出并验证了一个多模态基准：在受控的合作/竞争情境中，两名具身代理需同时整合语言、空间轨迹、凝视和面部表情进行交互，且系统性地操纵“心智理论（ToM）阶次”约束。对 13 个模型（含 11 个视觉-语言模型）在每模型 200 次试验的评估显示：大多数 VLM 无法生成与给定 ToM-阶次一致的行为；在施加明确 ToM-阶次约束时，行为也没有一致地随之改变。信号层面分析揭示两个连续瓶颈：1) 多数模型无法产生方向性连贯的非语言信号；2) 即便信号存在，模型也无法正确解读并做出反应。作为对照，带有显式 ToM 模块并将信念与动作耦合的 PCM-LLM 在所有条件下都表现良好，提示显式的信念—动作耦合可能是解决此类任务的充分成分。

Conclusion: 结论是：单纯的视觉-语言模型不足以实现由心智推理到协调言语/非言语行为的转换；显式建模信念并将其直接耦合到动作决策，是当前这类任务取得成功的关键。研究暗示需要在模型架构或训练范式上加入结构化的 ToM 能力和行为生成机制。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires agents to translate mental state inferences into coordinated behavioral signals across verbal and nonverbal channels simultaneously. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate theory of mind (ToM) reasoning and embodied behavior in isolation, leaving unmeasured the gap between social inference and social action. We introduce MOSAIC (Multimodal Orchestration of Social Action, Inference, and Communication), a controlled benchmark in which two embodied agents interact across cooperative and competitive scenarios requiring integration of verbal statements, spatial trajectories, gaze direction, and facial expression under systematically varied ToM constraints. Evaluating 13 models, including 11 VLMs, across 200 trials per model, we find that VLMs fail to produce behaviors consistent with the expected outcomes under ToM-order constraints, and that imposing explicit ToM-order constraints produces no reliable behavioral change aligned with the specified reasoning level. Signal-level analysis reveals two sequential bottlenecks: most models cannot produce directionally coherent nonverbal signals, and even when signals are present, VLM agents fail to interpret others behaviors and react to them. PCM-LLM, included as a structured architectural reference point with an explicit ToM module, succeeds across all conditions, suggesting that explicit belief-action coupling is a sufficient ingredient for this class of tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Don't Solve, Just Compare: Tiny Advisors for Runtime Intervention in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21027)
*Yanze Jiang,Mingxuan Li,Yuhao Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COTA：用一个小型“比较器”通过对比候选续航与主体提案来决定是否进行非强制性运行时干预，从而在不依赖强专家或高容量评论器的情况下提升LLM代理在长时序任务上的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失效检测不足以恢复执行，需要低成本的辅助机制提供恢复方向，避免依赖昂贵的专家解算器或高容量任务评论器。

Method: 训练一个小型比较器，利用相同前缀的反事实分支构建成对监督信号；比较采样得到的候选续航与演员提案，重复对比决定何时干预；若比较器偏好替代方案，则以非强制建议形式返回，允许演员重规划。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个任务及三种演员上，COTA在九种评估配置中均优于基线，表明即便辅助模型能力较弱也能有效改进代理表现。

Conclusion: 即便辅助模型远弱于主体模型，仅凭对比判断也能提供有用的恢复方向，COTA在多任务多主体设置中均显著提升性能，证明了构造性运行时干预的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as an important paradigm for real-world tasks that require reasoning, tool use, and sequential decision-making. As these agents operate over longer horizons, runtime intervention offers a way to improve reliability without retraining the underlying actor. Failure detection alone is insufficient. Effective intervention must also provide a useful direction for recovery. Existing approaches often rely on an expert solver or a critic that generates task-specific corrections, incurring either the cost of another capable solver or the capacity demands of a task-capable critic. We introduce Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor (COTA), a comparison-only framework for constructive runtime intervention. In COTA, a tiny comparator judges whether sampled alternatives lead to better continuations than the actor's proposal, and repeated comparisons determine when intervention is warranted. We train the comparator using pairwise supervision constructed from same-prefix counterfactual branches. Preferred alternatives are returned as non-binding advice, leaving the original actor to replan. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and tau^3-Retail with three actors, COTA improves all nine evaluation settings and outperforms the compared baselines. These results show that constructive runtime intervention can remain effective even when the auxiliary model has substantially weaker task-solving capability than the actor.

</details>


### [69] [Evaluating Large Language Model Performance on International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21036)
*Alexander Thomas,Hubert P. H. Shum,Darren Nellis,Manli Zhu,Phatpicha Yochum,William Bartle,Daniel Wrightson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DGEval是首个针对IMDG Amendment 42-24的LLM基准：模型可在查表类任务和有检索时提供帮助，但在装载/隔离/法规回忆等安全关键领域不可靠，因此必须有人类与权威源监督才能安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于IMDG规则复杂且更新频繁，行业从业者开始用LLMs作为决策支持，但缺乏系统评估以判定这些模型在安全关键的海运危险品合规任务中的可靠性。

Method: 构建DGEval基准：基于NCB Hazcheck专家编写的问题和危险品清单（DGL）条目，生成包含多项选择、开放式、DGL查表与法规识别任务的1678道题。对13个模型（含一个海事领域微调模型）在多种思考配置下进行评估，并测试网络检索对性能的影响。

Result: 在多项选择题上最好模型超过了人类从业者基线；网络检索提升了结构化查找任务的表现；但所有模型在装载、隔离与法规记忆类问题上表现最差，暴露出在运作性和法规回忆层面的不可接受不确定性。

Conclusion: 本文表明大型语言模型（LLMs）在结构化查表型任务（如DGL查找）和带网络检索时能部分支持危险品海运合规，但在关键的实务性区域（装载、隔离、法规条文回忆）表现不可靠，因而在任何安全关键部署前仍需人工复核与权威源验证。

Abstract: The transport of dangerous goods by sea is a high-consequence activity governed by the International Maritime Dangerous Goods (IMDG) Code, a complex regulatory framework where errors in classification, packaging, stowage, or segregation can result in fire, explosion, toxic release, or loss of life or vessel. Correct compliance requires accurately interpreting hundreds of pages of interacting provisions, updated on a two-year amendment cycle. Practitioners increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-support tools, yet no systematic evaluation exists of whether they can reliably interpret IMDG requirements for safety-critical use.
  This paper introduces DGEval, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM knowledge of IMDG Amendment 42-24. Built from expert-written questions on the NCB Hazcheck e-learning platform and structured lookups from the Dangerous Goods List (DGL), it comprises 1,678 questions across multiple-choice, open-ended, DGL lookup, and regulatory identification tasks. We evaluate 13 models from six providers across multiple thinking configurations, including one maritime domain-specific fine-tuned model, and test the effect of web search.
  Although the best-performing model exceeds the human practitioner baseline on multiple-choice questions, all models are weakest in the operationally safety-critical areas of stowage, segregation, and regulatory recall. These results indicate that LLMs may support compliance tasks, particularly structured DGL lookups with web search, but unreliability in operational areas and regulatory-text recall means human oversight and authoritative source verification remain necessary before deployment in any safety-critical context. DGEval is designed as a safety assurance instrument to be applied continuously as models evolve, not as a settled characterisation of current capability.

</details>


### [70] [Socialized Division and Collaboration: Rethinking Class-Incremental Learning under Optimization Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21044)
*Xinjie Yao,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Jiaqi Zhou,Dengyu Zhao,Zhoupeng Guo,Yan Fan,Guosong Jiang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SDC：一种基于Helmholtz自由能的会话—模型兼容性准则，用于在增量学习中以多模型分工+协作为应对优化冲突，替代单一模型范式以减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型在连续会话流中承受来自不同会话的冲突优化方向时会发生破坏性干扰与遗忘；这种问题源自将异质学习动力学强制压入同一参数空间的结构性局限，因而需要通过模型分工与协作来缓解。

Method: 设计并实现Socialized Division and Collaboration框架：1) 使用基于Helmholtz自由能的能量函数评估会话与现有模型的兼容性；2) 基于该兼容性衡量自适应地将新会话分配到已有专业模型或触发新模型的创建/演化；3) 在推理阶段实现模型间的协作/融合以生成最终预测。整个流程统一了会话分配、模型演化与协作推理。

Result: 提出了一个替代传统单体增量学习的整体框架，提供了一个原则性分配机制（基于自由能）来处理冲突目标并协调多个专业模型，展示了在存在持续优化冲突时的可行设计思路与潜在性能提升方向。

Conclusion: 本文提出将传统单模型的增量学习重构为多模型的划分与协作（SDC），以缓解因优化冲突导致的灾难性遗忘；通过能量基(session–model compatibility)准则指导会话分配与模型进化，从而在保持协同推理的同时避免将异质学习动力学强制压入单一参数空间。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning is commonly instantiated as a single-model paradigm, where a unified model sequentially adapts to an unbounded stream of sessions. While effective under mild distributional shifts, this formulation becomes strained when successive sessions induce incompatible optimization directions, leading to destructive interference and catastrophic forgetting. We argue that such forgetting reflects a structural limitation of enforcing heterogeneous learning dynamics within a single parameter space. Motivated by social solidarity theory, we propose Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC) as a reformulation of continual learning that decomposes session learning across specialized models in response to optimization conflicts, while enabling coordinated collaboration. To support this formulation with a principled allocation mechanism, we introduce an energy-based session-model compatibility criterion grounded in Helmholtz free energy, which guides adaptive session allocation and model evolution under conflicting objectives. This framework integrates session assignment, model evolution, and collaborative inference into a unified pipeline, offering an alternative to monolithic continual learning formulations and highlighting a broader design principle for learning under persistent optimization conflicts.

</details>


### [71] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 此文论证在强制形状约束（如单调性）时观察到的精度损失主要来自未被约束的自由特征及验证集划分，而非约束本身的固有代价。作者证明被约束类与去除某特征约束的类之间存在包含关系，从而对于任意风险度量，形状约束的额外风险P满足0 <= P <= D，其中D为去约束模型的超额风险。实证在一个野火严重度的序数分类任务上展示：坐标特征起到“屏蔽”作用，大幅降低D并使代价随验证协议变化显著。文中提出用于在训练前筛除不可识别实验的两步拟合检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当使用形状约束或物理先验的学习方法时，社区常将性能下降归因于对先验本身的代价。作者希望澄清实际来源，展示该代价常由未受约束自由特征及验证协议引起，从而帮助设计更可靠的评估流程和避免误判先验的实际效用。

Method: 理论上构建被约束假设类与消融假设类的包含关系，证明普适的不等式0<=P<=D；在真实数据集（N=26,681, K=3）上以四个气象驱动设为硬单调约束、空间坐标不受约束，使用从i.i.d.重采样到不同尺度空间阻塞的验证梯度评估影响，并通过反向应用证明下界来估计可解释分辨率；提出并验证两拟合（two-fit）筛选流程在训练前排除不可识别案例。

Result: - 理论：证明对于任意风险度量与任意数据分布，形状约束的额外风险P被忽略相应特征（消融）模型的额外风险D上界，即0<=P<=D。- 实证：在野火严重度任务上发现坐标特征能“屏蔽”先验，显著降低D并使先验成本对验证协议高度敏感；不同空间阻塞尺度可将D从0.0942降至0.0050。- 实用：提出两拟合筛选法在训练前识别不可识别的实验；通过318次比较确定自校准宏-F1下限为0.0220。

Conclusion: 强制形状或物理先验的精度成本并非先验固有属性，而主要由自由特征与验证协议决定；通过数学包含关系，证明任何情形下先验引入的超额风险被其同源消融模型的超额风险所上界。实证表明验证划分（尤其空间阻塞尺度）和自由坐标对该上界影响巨大，并提出实用的两拟合筛选方法来避免不可识别的试验。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [72] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CellPath-Bench is a large-scale cellular-resolution benchmark that measures and compares how well frozen pathology foundation models encode and transfer cell-type information using nucleus-anchored linear probes, revealing pronounced differences across models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks do not systematically evaluate whole-slide pathology foundation models (PFMs) at cellular resolution—specifically their ability to encode (decode) cell-type information and to transfer that information across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. A standardized, high-throughput framework is needed to audit frozen PFM representations at the nucleus level.

Method: Quality-controlled selection of Xenium datasets; creation of 25 spatially registered H&E–Xenium sections; harmonization into fine/coarse taxonomies; extraction of frozen WSI feature maps at nucleus coordinates; standardized multiclass linear probes; metrics CRA (within-section advantage vs patch-mean pooling) and CRT (generalization across sections/datasets/organs); large-scale automated evaluation of 30 models across many conditions.

Result: Introduced CellPath-Bench: curated 25 spatially aligned H&E–Xenium tissue sections (after QC of 52 candidates) covering 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized taxonomies, and a protocol to sample frozen WSI feature maps at nuclear coordinates. Defined metrics: Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) and Cell Representation Transferability (CRT). Benchmarked 30 PFMs with 304,920 runs across magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and protocols, revealing substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and cross-domain generalization.

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench provides a standardized, reproducible framework to audit cellular information in frozen PFMs, enabling multidimensional capability profiling and guiding model selection and future model development.

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [73] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过实证测试和问卷调查揭示LLM在印度合同法应用中存在“高置信度错判”问题，提出教学与系统性验证改进以降低职业与制度风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM在印度法律语境中对判例/成文法变更的处理能力，识别高置信度错误带来的职业与制度性风险，并探索人类在面对机器幻觉时的脆弱性与适应性。

Method: 两阶段社会技术审计：Phase I 用60例涉及1872年合同法修订的案例测试三款LLM（ChatGPT/GPT-5.2、Meta AI、Perplexity AI），并定义并计算高置信度错误率（HCER，置信度≥9/10且裁决错误）。Phase II 对380名印度法律学生进行问卷调查，衡量他们对机器幻觉的识别、验证行为与AI伦理培训背景。

Result: 所有模型在应对法定更新上表现不佳：Meta AI HCER=31.7%、Perplexity=15.0%、ChatGPT=6.7%；学生中遭遇伪造引证者更倾向于进行验证（4.2/5 vs 2.8/5），但71.1%未接受AI伦理培训，表明制度与教育不足。

Conclusion: 研究指出将LLM引入印度司法有助于司法可达性但伴随严重风险，尤其是模型在高置信度下给出错误裁决的“信心惰性”现象，并提出预防性教育与系统层面的溯源/验证改进建议。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [74] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，当把大型语言模型(LLM)用作“裁判”时，其对“可信度”（trust）与“真值判定”（truth）之间的区分比假定的更弱，特别是在来源标签（人类/AI）改变时，信任分数不仅改变，连事实二元判定和模型内部的正确概率也会被影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见做法把LLM输出的多维评价（如可信度、可靠性、事实性）视为相互独立的证据。本研究检验这种独立性的合理性，聚焦于信任评分与事实判定这对常见评价维度。

Method: 1) 在“正确率受控”的问答（QA）集上评估LLM作为裁判时对信任分数与二元真值判定的对应关系，并与人类行为参考比较；2) 应用压力测试：仅改变相同QA的来源提示（Human vs AI），测量信任分数、二元真值判定及基于logit的正确侧概率的变化。

Result: LLM裁判在受控正确性条件下比人类表现出更紧密的信任-真值耦合；在来源提示被置换（Human↔AI）时，不仅信任分数显著变化，二元真值判定和logit衍生的“更可能正确”概率也随之改变，表明信任分数并非独立证据。

Conclusion: 目前的LLM-as-Judge流程不应把信任分数当作独立于真值判定的证据；来源提示会同时扭曲信任与真伪判定。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [75] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReFrame在不修改或访问下游模型的前提下，通过两阶段的本地证据生成与提示/图像重写路由，改进多模态模型的测试时安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态大模型（MLLMs）在部署后难以对齐安全性的挑战，特别是在不允许重训练或访问内部状态的闭源/已部署模型场景下，针对跨模态越狱、缺乏安全感知与过度拒绝问题提出可行的测试时（test-time）对齐方法。

Method: 设计两代理流程：1) 证据生成代理使用轻量本地MLLM从原始多模态输入提取互补的风险证据与效用证据；2) 重写与路由代理将这些证据汇总为安全代理提示（proxy prompt）并决定是否以及如何路由/修改图像，然后调用下游模型以获取响应。全过程不训练下游模型、不访问内部状态，并在多个MLLM与基准上验证了越狱防御、安全感知提升与过敏性拒绝减少的效果。

Result: 提出ReFrame——一个无需重训练的多模态输入重构框架：利用同一本地轻量MLLM扮演两个代理（证据生成与重写/路由），在调用下游闭源MLLM前构造风险/效用证据并转换为安全替代提示与图像路由，从而提升越狱抵抗、安全感知并减少过敏性拒绝，同时保持多模态效用。

Conclusion: 测试时输入重构是对闭源多模态模型进行安全对齐的有效路径；通过显式构建风险与效用证据并据此重写提示与路由输入，能在不牺牲任务效用的前提下增强越狱防护和安全感知，但其效果受本地轻量模型质量和证据生成策略限制，仍需在自适应对抗、效率与形式化保障方面作进一步研究。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [76] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将差异图发现方法扩展到线性离散时间序列，用以识别在异常期因果系数发生变化的变量（作用违背根因），并在仿真与真实监控数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To identify mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems, specifically variables whose causal role changes (effect-defying root causes) between normal and anomalous regimes.

Method: Formalize root-cause analysis as detecting changes in causal coefficients under linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models, and adapt several existing difference-graph discovery methods (originally for two populations) to the time-series setting by treating normal vs anomalous regimes as the two populations; evaluate on simulated data and on IT and intensive-care monitoring datasets.

Result: On simulations the adapted methods recover changed causal links; on real IT and ICU monitoring data they provide actionable localization of causal mechanisms underlying anomalies.

Conclusion: Difference graph discovery can be adapted to linear discrete-time time-series models to localize effect-defying root causes: variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime.

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [77] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A norm-based token presence gate p_i integrated into attention and layer operators produces exact null-token algebra and compositional closure with minimal empirical disruption in a small retrofit; further and broader evaluations are required to support general claims.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Masks operate at relation level and cannot represent a non-participating token state carried by representations. A representation-carried presence coefficient would allow exact null-token insertion and compositional preservation of 'missing' states across layers.

Method: Define p_i = ||h_i||^2/(τ+||h_i||^2). Implement OAttention that multiplies receiver outputs by p_i and weights sources by p_j in numerator and partition of attention; derive paired O-components (OFFN, ONorm, OInject, OStandardize) and the O-Closure law M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0. Validate via contract tests, a GPU evaluation, a zero-fine-tuning retrofit on a pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor (measuring RMSE changes), and a two-block ablation.

Result: The construction gives exact algebraic properties (zero element, null-receiver/source insertion, empty-support properties). In empirical retrofit tests, calibrated-hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants changed mean RMSE by +0.088% and +0.177% respectively over 18 matched cases. Ablation shows OAttention alone does not preserve NULL through ordinary host components; the full OTransformer path is required. Authors note scoped limits of the tests.

Conclusion: Introducing a token-level active-presence coefficient p_i and using it to gate emissions and to weight sources in attention yields an operator family (OAttention, OTransformer, and auxiliary O-components) that enforces algebraic null-token behavior and compositional closure, while retaining standard attention mechanics.

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [78] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTRY用检索增强的文本信号+结构化特征喂入XGBoost，保持可解释性与确定性，在实务数据上显著优于问卷流程，但需关注标签质量、时序验证、检索组件可审计性与部署/监控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自报问卷的变更风险评估主观、易被规避且难以区分常规变更与未来导致重大事故的变更，因而需要更准确、一致和可审计的评估方法以满足大型金融机构的合规需求。

Method: 构建混合检索（语义+词汇）从历史变更请求中检索相似文本，提取风险信号并压缩为单一标量；与结构化运维元数据、应用依赖图和事故历史一起输入基于XGBoost的确定性模型；使用SHAP提供逐预测解释。

Result: 在企业级变更数据集上，SENTRY达成ROC AUC=0.87、总体准确率=85%，并将高风险变更的识别率提升约3.25倍；并讨论了架构权衡与合规场景下机器学习的适用性。

Conclusion: 作者提出的SENTRY将问卷式风险评估替换为可解释、确定性的机器学习流水线，结合检索增强生成（RAG）提取非结构化变更请求中的风险信号并将其压缩为单值特征，保留了基于XGBoost的SHAP可解释性。在企业级数据上取得了0.87 ROC AUC、85%准确率，并能以3.25倍的效率识别高风险变更。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [79] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出P3Bench用于评估个性化隐私，发现提示式方法易忽视个性化策略，Repair（注意力头干预）在推理时能有效提升隐私遵从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具代理性的LLM能访问多样的用户数据，信息披露的可接受边界在相同情境下也会因用户而异，传统情境隐私研究忽视了这一用户差异性，因而需要将用户特定偏好纳入隐私控制。

Method: 工作构建了P3Bench基准，将情境隐私策略扩展为包含用户特定披露偏好的个性化隐私策略；通过在多模型（如Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma3-4B）上以提示式策略做对比评估其政策遵从性；提出Repair方法，在推理时对模型注意力头进行干预以调整输出，减少违反个性化隐私策略的情况。

Result: 实验表明，提示式策略在执行个性化隐私方面表现欠佳，Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均政策忽视率分别为51.25%和74.28%；应用Repair后，模型对用户隐私偏好的遵从性显著提高，违反策略的情况显著减少（论文中报告了定量改进）。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：在具代理性的AI场景下，仅有情境隐私不足以保护用户，需引入基于用户偏好的个性化隐私；传统基于提示的策略无法可靠执行个性化隐私策略；提出的Repair（推理时的注意力头干预）能显著提升模型对用户隐私偏好的遵从性。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [80] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PSL is a dual-view evidence conversion framework that extracts stance and graph-structure signals from political records to improve LLM-based predictive political QA, yielding consistent gains over baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM augmentation treats external political resources as purely knowledge-based evidence, failing to model prediction-relevant signals like actor-specific stances and indirect actor dependencies; the work aims to surface these complementary signals for better predictive reasoning.

Method: Dual-view framework: (1) Semantic view extracts question-relevant stances from actor records to produce stance evidence for LLM prompting; (2) Vector/view learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph to capture high-order dependencies; both views are combined to augment LLM inference.

Result: Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baseline augmentation methods; ablation studies show both stance and structure components contribute complementary gains.

Conclusion: PSL (Probabilistic Stance and Linkage?) demonstrates consistent improvements on predictive political QA by converting semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence, leveraging both actor stance signals and high-order structure signals.

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [81] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个带有本体支撑的AI模型交换平台，旨在工业场景下改善模型/数据的发现、交换与管理，并通过实时关键系统用例展示其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中存在大量需追踪和管理的AI相关资产，且需透明比较与发现相关资产以支持工程实践；当前缺乏统一的语义与交换机制，导致资产共享与复用困难。

Method: 设计并实现一个支持模型使用、交换与分析的平台；将一个本体（ontology）嵌入平台以统一语义描述；通过具体的工业用例（实时关键系统）对平台功能与价值进行展示与说明。

Result: 提出的平台和本体提供了一种可行的路径来解决资产管理与语义一致性问题；用例展示了在实时关键系统场景下平台的适用性与潜在益处，但论文可能在评估量化与标准对比上仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 提出并实现了一个集成本体的AI模型交换平台，旨在在工业情境下减少语义鸿沟、增强模型与数据资产的可发现性、可追踪性和管理性，并通过实时关键系统的用例演示其实用性。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [82] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将GPX划分阶段重写为图并行问题，在GPU上通过合并父代路径、拆分四度顶点、删除公共边和连通分量识别并行实现，实现对10k–2M城市实例的显著加速（48x–625x）并降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GA的优秀TSP解法（如EAX/GPX）在大规模问题与多核并行架构上受限，因交叉操作的内存非规则访问、图遍历和串行依赖导致可扩展性不足。

Method: 将GPX划分转化为图并行任务，采用共聚内存布局、ghost-node变换和并行连通分量分析；在CUDA上并行化父代路径并并（union）、四度点拆分、公共边删除以及重组合分量识别。

Result: 在10k到2M城市实例上，相较于朴素的串行CPU实现，GPU划分实现获得48x–625x加速并显著降低内存开销，证明了运算子级并行对可扩展性的促进作用。

Conclusion: 操作符级并行（尤其GPX划分）能够显著提升GA基TSP求解器在多核GPU上的可扩展性，本文展示了在大规模实例上的高加速比和内存效率。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [83] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLEAR introduces a continuous gating mechanism to route a safety LoRA conditionally based on hidden states, improving the safety–utility trade-off: strong harm reduction with much smaller utility loss than global safety tuning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Global safety tuning (e.g., SFT or LoRA applied uniformly) can reduce harmful outputs but often degrades utility on benign prompts by modifying the backbone indiscriminately. A conditional mechanism is needed to activate safety interventions only when necessary.

Method: Introduce a low-overhead safety low-rank adapter and a continuous gate computed from hidden states that scales the adapter activation per example/token; freeze backbone weights and train the adapter and gate so safety behavior is applied selectively. Evaluate on standard safety and utility benchmarks (e.g., HarmBench, GSM8K) and compare to global SFT and LoRA baselines.

Result: CLEAR, which uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter, markedly reduces harmful completions while retaining much of the base model's utility. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR cuts HarmBench ASR from 32.3% to 0.5% and achieves up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than global SFT or LoRA.

Conclusion: Conditional continuous routing of a lightweight safety adapter is a promising way to improve model alignment, achieving strong robustness on safety benchmarks without the utility degradation common to globally applied safety tuning.

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [84] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUSO progressively internalizes and selectively uses skills via action-level RL signals, yielding better performance and generalization compared to prior static or trajectory-level skill methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skills serve different roles as a policy matures—initially as learnable guidance, then as capability supports, and finally as optional decision tools—while existing methods treat skill use statically or rely on noisy trajectory-level signals, failing to provide action-differentiated training.

Method: Progressive three-stage reinforcement-learning-based method: (1) early joint learning combining teacher/skill guidance and environment outcomes to acquire foundational skills; (2) emphasis shift to outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving; (3) action-level evaluation comparing each sampled action under skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts, combining this signal with trajectory advantage to upweight beneficial skill-sensitive actions and suppress harmful ones.

Result: Empirical improvements on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA: AUSO consistently outperforms competitive baselines and yields better out-of-distribution generalization; action-level signals enable more targeted skill integration.

Conclusion: AUSO unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization pipeline, allowing skills to transition from external supervision to adaptive decision knowledge and improving agent performance and OOD generalization.

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [85] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了数据提取指令与法规模糊性对LLM生成合规文档的影响，结论是：严格格式提高一致性但可能增加幻觉；宽松格式需更丰富上下文与提示设计以保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EU法规（如ESPR与GDPR）要求文档化合规工件，但工业数据异构且分散、法规表述存在模糊性，使人工作成困难。研究者提议用LLM自动生成，但未充分研究数据提取与法规模糊性如何影响输出质量。

Method: 比较不同大语言模型（LLM）在生成合规文档（DPP/DBP、DPIA）时的表现：通过改变数据提取指令与提示上下文的严格性，生成合规工件并与人工制定的基线schema进行对比评估，衡量一致性、完整性与幻觉率。

Result: 实验发现：在格式/规范较宽松（如DPIA）时，模型需更多上下文与严格提示才能保持一致性与完整性；在格式严格（如DBP）时，结果更一致但幻觉问题增加。不同模型表现随提示设计与上下文信息量显著波动。

Conclusion: LLM可辅助合规工件生成，但需结合严格的schema、检索增强、提示工程与人工验证以降低幻觉与提高可审计性；建议引入结构化输出约束、事实校验和领域专家循环反馈。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [86] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在GCS上提出Steiner-TSP的精确求解框架：以有根回路前缀的分支定界为搜索骨架，结合加法下界图与连通流放松作为下界证据，给出终止性与ε近似证书，并在移动操作器检查任务上验证了方法的可行性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在连续空间（凸集）中访问多个目标的Steiner-TSP类问题：状态空间无限、允许中转点与重复访问，且需联合路径、动作模式与逻辑先后约束，迫切需要有界且可证的搜索/优化框架。

Method: 基于根回路前缀的统一分支定界：1) 对已承诺前缀用加法下界图成本进行界定；2) 对剩余目标及回根问题用切分分离的连通流(relaxed connected-flow)松弛来下界；3) 搜索策略包括best-first与depth-first，分别在不同条件下给出有限终止性；4) 通过全局下界给出ε近似证书；5) 可处理LTL_f表达的先后约束与联合感知-轨迹选择问题。

Result: 本文的主要成果是：在凸集图(Graphs of Convex Sets, GCS)上形式化Steiner旅行商问题；提出基于有根回路前缀的统一分支定界搜索；设计加法下界图成本与切分分离的连通流放松以下界剩余访问代价；证明在一致正成本假设下，最佳优先遍历在无初始可行解时和深度优先在有有限初始可行解时会以有限次数展开终止；给出ε因子下的全局下界证书；将方法扩展到移动操作器的联合感知模式、访问顺序与连续轨迹选择，并支持LTL_f先行约束；实验显示两种遍历策略在所有基准实例上30s内均找到可行解，平均认证最优差分别为28.1%和29.7%，而两种近期基线仅能在约一半实例上成功。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于将连续空间访问问题（GCS）纳入Steiner-TSP形式化，并提出理论上有保证的分支定界策略与下界构造，兼顾可行性证明与近似认证；实验证明方法鲁棒且比几项基线更可靠，但平均认证差距仍较大，暗示下界或启发式仍有改进空间。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [87] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出把解剖先验（软约束与硬结构）并入物理/学习框架（AINN），用SE(3)+Cosserat杆+单边接触+Wasserstein投影从2D造影约束3D血管变形；完成初步在硅验证，但需数值收敛、真实数据实验和端到端训练以证明其实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽数值合理但可能产生解剖不可能的输出，且在数据匮乏时泛化性差；引入医学解剖先验可强制解剖一致性并减少对大量数据的依赖。

Method: 将血管中心线与导丝路径提升到SE(3)的帧曲线，使用Cosserat杆模型耦合导丝与扭曲调制且以解剖锚固的血管，通过单边腔体接触不等式实现非穿透约束；预测为耦合弹性能量的约束极小化（接触力为拉格朗日乘子）；监督采用Wasserstein-2在C臂投影空间的损失，使2D血管造影可训练3D预测。

Result: 运动学、投影与损失在已知真值下完成验证；力学求解仅通过其最优性条件验证；位移预测尚未达到网格收敛；当前未训练网络，计划将模型迁移到真实CT以评估是否提升预测精度并降低数据需求。

Conclusion: AINN框架能将软/硬解剖先验整合入神经网络与力学优化中，用于受限数据下的血管变形预测，但当前仍停留在体外/在硅验证阶段，力学求解与数值收敛、真实影像验证和端到端训练尚未完成。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [88] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIALS benchmark (161 tasks) exposes that current V+L models fail to match expert interpretation of real-life scientific images, indicating limited immediate utility in professional life-science workflows and motivating domain-specific model development.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing V+L models are evaluated on natural images or polished figures; however, professional life-science workflows rely on raw, varied visual artifacts whose interpretation is central to decision-making—so a targeted benchmark is needed to measure models' utility in this domain.

Method: Construct VIALS, a benchmark of 161 VQA-style interpretation tasks covering real, workflow-level scientific images; evaluate current vision-language models on these tasks and compare with expert human performance.

Result: State-of-the-art V+L models produce fluent but often incorrect interpretations on VIALS, while domain experts perform well, demonstrating a gap in domain knowledge and visual reasoning for scientific artifacts.

Conclusion: Frontier vision-language models cannot reliably interpret professional life-science visual artifacts; domain expertise and domain-specific visual reasoning are required for accurate interpretation.

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Vibe Coding: Practice, Performance, Productivity, and Risk -A State-of-the-Art Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20446)
*Dominik L. Michels,Mutaz Abu Ghazaleh,Francois Lazzari,Nabil Kassem,Jonathan Klein*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Early 'vibe coding' shows clear generative strengths but mixed productivity and notable risks; apparent contradictions in the literature are consistent once measurement differences are controlled; gains likely concentrated on new code, with reduced or negative effects on mature codebases.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Compile and reconcile early empirical evidence on 'vibe coding' (AI-assisted development by intent-and-run) to guide researchers, practitioners and policymakers facing contradictory results and emergent harms.

Method: Systematic state-of-the-art review across disciplines: literature search, task-level performance synthesis, cross-study comparison of productivity metrics, qualitative analysis of security/code-quality/legal findings, and formulation of a falsifiable conjecture.

Result: Benchmarks saturated but uneven task capabilities (good at generation, weak at fault detection/documentation); conflicting productivity measures explained by method/scope/time-horizon; documented security failures, code-quality degradation, copyright exposure, and skill atrophy; proposed patterns explaining dispersion and a falsifiable conjecture about differential gains on new vs mature code.

Conclusion: The evidence supports targeted benefits of vibe coding for new-development tasks but warns of risks (security, maintainability, legal, human skill). Future research should use standardized, longitudinal, and independently measured protocols to test the conjecture that benefits shrink on mature codebases.

Abstract: Vibe coding - AI-assisted software development in which the developer describes intent in natural language and validates results by running rather than reading the generated code - was named by Andrej Karpathy in February 2025 and produced its first body of empirical evidence within seventeen months. This state-of-the-art review assembles that evidence across a cross-disciplinary corpus spanning software engineering, human-computer interaction, labour economics, security research, governance, and education. We survey the model landscape, the tool ecosystem, and the performance record by task type, finding the early benchmarks saturated but task-level capability uneven: reliable code generation alongside weak fault detection and hard-to-audit documentation. The productivity record is at first contradictory: peer-reviewed field experiments report +26% more tasks per week, independent randomised trials measure a 19% slowdown, and team-level telemetry shows code-review time up +441%. We argue these readings are consistent once measurement method, scope, and time horizon are held constant, and identify six patterns behind the dispersion, among them effect-shrinkage under broader measurement, self-report diverging from independent measurement, output volume conflated with productivity, and bold claims walked back once tested over longer horizons. We further document security failures in deployed applications, code-quality degradation visible in large-scale code and developer telemetry, unsettled copyright exposure, and evidence of skill atrophy. The review closes with the open research questions and one falsifiable conjecture: that the gains are real on new code and shrink or reverse on mature codebases, which would account for most of the disagreement in the record.

</details>


### [90] [Making Deployments Safe at Meta: Health Checks for Continuous Change-Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20513)
*Prakash KL,Anton Korenkov,Uttam Thakore,Christopher Hegre*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 介绍Meta在持续部署中用于阻止回归的Service Health Checker：系统设计、模板化检查构建、与分阶段发布的联动、运营中遇到的问题及解决方案，以及面向AI调优的未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模持续部署环境中，如何在发布速度和系统可靠性之间取得平衡，自动化检测并在回滚前阻止回归成为核心需求。Meta需要一种可扩展、防止回归的部署时健康检查基础设施，以覆盖成千上万个异构服务并与分阶段发布集成。

Method: 构建分布式的实时/近实时健康检查服务，支持模板化的指标查询（通过已有监控指标）、阈值配置与复杂谓词表达式，集成至分层/分阶段发布流程，实现基于策略的自动回滚。运维上采用测量（误报率、检测延时等）、工具改进（更好的默认值、去噪、告警抑制）和完整性测试来解决规模化挑战。

Result: 提出并实践了名为Service Health Checker的基于预防的分布式系统架构；提供了可复用的模板化指标查询、阈值和工作流谓词的组合方式；将健康检查与分层/分阶段滚动发布集成，实现对回归的自动回滚；并在运营过程中识别并缓解了噪声、告警疲劳、漂移和未覆盖回归等问题，通过测量、工具改进和更好默认值得到缓解。还讨论了面向未来的方向，如AI辅助的健康检查调优。

Conclusion: Service Health Checker是一个可在大规模生产环境中部署的、以预防为导向的健康检查体系，能在维持高发布频率的同时降低回归风险。但其有效性依赖于良好的度量、合理的默认值和持续的运维投入。AI辅助调优为下一步可行方向，但需注意自动化带来的风险与监控成本。

Abstract: Continuous deployment to large scale production systems creates a tension between release velocity and reliability. Every change is a potential reliability incident, yet every delay is a missed opportunity. This paper describes the deployment time health check infrastructure that Meta uses to mediate this tension across thousands of heterogeneous services. We summarize the architecture of this prevention based distributed system's service called Service Health Checker, explain how check authors compose templated metric queries, thresholds, and workflow predicates; and discuss how the system is integrated with tiered and phased rollouts so that regressions trigger automatic rollback. We then describe the operational problems that emerged at scale, such as noise, alert fatigue, drift, and uncovered regressions, and the program of measurement, tooling, and improved defaults we deployed to address them. We close with lessons learned from years of operating deployment health checks at Meta, and the directions we are exploring next, including AI assisted health check tuning. Index Terms: deployment safety, continuous deployment, monitoring, software reliability, release engineering, software reliability engineering, AIOps, anomaly detection

</details>


### [91] [Testing and Evaluation of Agentic AI Systems In Military Command and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20597)
*Ulysse Richard,Heather Frase,Sarah Cao,Di Cooke,Sebastian Kwon,Adrianna Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agentic properties undermine core assumptions behind existing T&E methods for C2 AI, breaking the warrant from tests to fielded behavior; narrower, conditional assurance claims remain feasible but shift evidentiary burden into deployment and require new methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether public commitments to rigorous testing and human oversight for agentic AI in military command-and-control can be supported by defensible assurance cases, and to identify where established T&E methods fail.

Method: Structured review of 240 documented Testing and Evaluation (T&E) practices across eight evaluation dimensions and three lifecycle stages, mapping assumptions to agentic properties and deriving assurance claims.

Result: Identified eight assumptions (grouped into system specifiability, stability, composability, supervisability) that established methods make; showed agentic properties weaken all eight, eroding the argument linking evidence to claims. Derived ten assurance claims for first three clusters; found documented methods do not support broad system-level behavior claims but can, in principle with mature methods, recover narrower claims (bounded mission envelopes, trajectory-grounded correctness, executable runtime constraints, characterized run-to-run variance).

Conclusion: Current documented T&E record cannot justify broad system-level assurances for agentic C2 systems. Recoverable assurance requires mature methods, bounded operational envelopes, runtime constraints, and governance for residual uncertainty (expiry conditions, ownership); supervisability evidence depends on stability and human-factors T&E beyond scope.

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are being procured for military command and control (C2) under public commitments to rigorous testing and human oversight. Whether such commitments can be discharged depends on their supporting assurance case, which requires three elements: claims specifying the conditions for acceptability, evidence bearing on those claims, and an argument connecting the two. Through a structured review of 240 documented Testing and Evaluation (T&E) practices, spanning eight evaluation dimensions and three lifecycle stages, we identify eight assumptions that established methods make about their test article, grouped into four clusters: system specifiability, stability, composability, and supervisability. Agentic properties weaken all eight assumptions. This erosion affects the argument connecting evidence to claims, not the claims or evidence themselves. As a result, test results may satisfy process requirements, but they do not warrant the inference from tested to fielded behavior.
  We derive ten assurance claims for the first three assumption clusters and assess whether current and emerging methods can address each, mapping operational consequences through five C2 scenarios. Supervisability is identified but not assessed here, since evidencing it depends on system stability results and human factors T&E methods beyond the present scope. The documented record does not support broad claims about system-level behavior, but narrower claims remain recoverable in principle, contingent on mature methods: bounded mission envelopes, trajectory-grounded correctness, executable runtime constraints, and characterized run-to-run variance. Part of the evidentiary burden shifts into deployment, making the determination to field a continuing act. Where evidence cannot be generated, the residual uncertainty can be governed through defined expiry conditions and assigned ownership.

</details>


### [92] [Behavior Specification-Guided Program Synthesis for Binary Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20628)
*Kangchen Zhu,Shangwen Wang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhouyang Jia,Xiaoling Li,Jun Ma,Jie Yu,Xiaoguang Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinMirror 将二进制反混淆重构为以行为规范驱动的程序合成问题，通过动态执行轨迹与交互快照作为规范，合成并验证源代码，从而在保留运行时语义方面显著优于基于反编译的结构性变换方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦源代码级反混淆或先反编译再做结构性恢复，但反编译会丢失类型等高层语义，导致产出代码质量差且不能保证保留运行时行为；因此需要一种直接基于可观测运行行为来恢复可读、高质量源码的方法。

Method: 将被混淆的二进制在受控环境下运行以收集动态执行轨迹和交互快照，作为行为规范输入到程序合成器；合成器产出高层源代码并通过与原始二进制的运行时观测比对进行验证与筛选，最终输出通过行为测试的恢复源码。

Result: 在 1.5M 个合成混淆二进制上评估，BinMirror 在极端混淆条件下达成 unit-test Pass@1 为 74.5%，明显优于现有基线，表明其在恢复语义清晰度方面的实用效果。

Conclusion: 以可观察行为作为约束的合成范式能比反编译后结构变换更可靠地恢复二进制程序的语义，BinMirror 在大规模合成数据上表现出明显优势，证明行为驱动方法在二进制反混淆任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Deobfuscation is critical to reverse engineering and security analysis because it restores the readability and analyzability of obfuscated code. However, existing research primarily focuses on source-code deobfuscation, while binary-level deobfuscation remains largely underexplored despite its practical importance when source code is unavailable. Existing binary deobfuscation methods typically decompile binaries into pseudocode and then apply structural transformations. However, because compilation discards high-level semantics such as precise type information and source-level structures, this decompilation-based paradigm often produces low-quality code and provides limited assurance that the recovered code preserves the runtime behavior of the original program. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift from structural transformation to behavior-driven synthesis. Our core insight is that although obfuscation distorts a program's internal structure, semantics-preserving transformations must retain its observable execution behavior. Based on this insight, we introduce BinMirror, an approach that reformulates binary deobfuscation as a behavior-specification-guided program synthesis task. By treating dynamic execution traces and interaction snapshots as behavioral specifications, BinMirror synthesizes high-quality source code and validates it against runtime observations collected from heavily obfuscated binaries. Extensive evaluations on 1.5 million synthetically obfuscated binaries show that BinMirror significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a unit-test Pass@1 of 74.5% under extreme obfuscation. These results demonstrate the practical utility of BinMirror in restoring semantic clarity for real-world security analysis.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Understanding Operating System Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20643)
*Hongyao Zuo,Jiali Li,Jiajun Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Study of 1,500 defects in Android, Linux, HarmonyOS reveals where and how OS defects occur, their severity, triggers, and repair patterns; offers guidelines to improve defect detection and debugging.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understand characteristics of OS defects to improve detection and debugging; ensure OS quality across systems.

Method: Collected 1,500 defects from three OSes; classified defects by OS layer, affected functions, triggering conditions, severity, and repaired code elements; performed joint-dimension and cross-OS similarity analyses; summarized findings and derived implications.

Result: Large-scale analysis of 1,500 defects from Android, Linux, HarmonyOS; identified distributions across layers, functions, triggers, severity, patched code elements; cross-OS comparisons and joint-dimension analysis; actionable implications provided.

Conclusion: Comprehensive findings about OS defect distributions and repair patterns across three OSes inform targeted detection/debugging strategies and suggest future research directions.

Abstract: Operating systems (OS) serve as the foundation for all other software systems, and thus defects in OSes can lead to severe conquences, such as system crashes and data corruption, affecting billions of users. This broad impact underscores the necessity and importance of ensuring OS quality. Understanding the characteristics of OS defects is a fundamental step in this quality assurance task, as it facilitates the design of effective defect detection and debugging approaches. In this work, we conduct a large-scale study of 1,500 defects from three distinct and representative operating systems (Android, Linux, and HarmonyOS) spanning both mobile and desktop environments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest study of its kind in this domain. By analyzing the distribution of OS defects across multiple classification dimensions, including the OS layer where defects occur, the functions they affect, how they are triggered, their severity, and the code elements involved in their repair, as well as performing joint analysis between dimensions and cross-OS similarity comparisons, we summarize several major findings that contribute to a comprehensive understanding of OS defects across systems.
